**How the gap is possible.** ✅ **Nihilation** (*néantisation*): consciousness stands back from what is and negates it — questioning, imagining alternatives, projecting beyond. The for-itself is "the being by which **nothingness** comes into the world".

**The consequence.** ⚠️ The for-itself perpetually desires to become en-soi while remaining conscious — a **pour-soi-en-soi**, which is God, and a contradiction. Human reality is defined by this impossible desire: "man is a **useless passion**".

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** ten marks reward precision, not coverage; the two formulas and the impossible synthesis are the whole examinable content.

**Exam-length execution — 10 marks, about 150 to 220 words:** thesis one line; en-soi three; pour-soi four; nihilation two; consequence two.

- **Structure:** thesis → in-itself → for-itself → mechanism → consequence.
- **Doctrine:** self-identity; nihilation; the contradictory ideal.
- **Evidence:** *Being and Nothingness* (1943).
- **Distinction:** ontological, not mind–matter.
- **Criticism:** implicit — the coherence of non-self-identity, if space allows.

### ORIGINAL MAINS PRACTICE WITH MODEL SOLUTIONS

> Six original questions in the style of the paper — two each at 10, 15 and 20 marks — covering the parts of the clause the 2018–2025 corpus has not yet examined directly: Kierkegaardian anxiety, the "they" and falling, the Look, indirect communication, the fear/anxiety comparison, and the movement-level objections. Every model answer is independent learner practice.

#### Original 1 · 10 marks

**Question:** “Anxiety is the dizziness of freedom.” Explain what Kierkegaard means by anxiety and why he distinguishes it from fear.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** Anxiety is not an intense fear but an **objectless** disclosure of possibility, and it is the objectlessness that gives it its philosophical role.

**The distinction.** ✅ **Fear** is directed at a **determinate entity within the world** — a dog, an examination, a fall — and it drives one back toward the world, seeking shelter in the familiar. **Anxiety** (*Angest*), as the pseudonym **Vigilius Haufniensis** analyses it in *The Concept of Anxiety* (**1844**), has no such object: "the object of anxiety is a **nothing**".

**What it discloses.** Since there is no entity for anxiety to be about, what it discloses cannot be an entity but must be a **structure**: **possibility**, "the possibility of being able". Hence the formula — anxiety is "the **dizziness of freedom**" — and its description as "a sympathetic antipathy and an antipathetic sympathy".

**The example.** The man at the edge of an abyss grows dizzy, but **the dizziness is as much in the eye as in the abyss**: what appals is not the drop but the discovery that nothing prevents him.

**Its role.** ⚠️ Anxiety is already present **in innocence**, before any knowledge of good and evil, and is the **presupposition** of the leap by which sin and selfhood enter — not its cause, since a caused leap would not be a leap. ⚠️ Lowrie renders *Angest* as "dread" and Hong as "anxiety".

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** the whole answer is one distinction, stated in kind rather than degree, and defended by an example.

- **Structure:** thesis → distinction → what is disclosed → example → role → translation caution.
- **Doctrine:** objectlessness; possibility; the presupposition of the leap.
- **Evidence:** *The Concept of Anxiety* (1844), Vigilius Haufniensis.
- **Distinction:** difference in kind, not intensity.
- **Criticism:** implicit in the caution about translations and attribution.

#### Original 2 · 10 marks

**Question:** Explain Heidegger's notion of *das Man* and show why 'falling' is not a moral failing.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** *Das Man* names the impersonal "anyone" in whose name things are done, and falling is the ontological structure of absorption in that anonymity — which is why neither carries blame.

**The "they".** ✅ Dasein exists with others — **being-with** (*Mitsein*, §26) is constitutive, not added on — and tends to lose itself in what "**one**" (*man*) does, says and thinks. ⚠️ *Das Man* is not "other people" but the anonymous norm-setter: one reads what one reads, one retires when one retires, and nobody in particular is answerable.

**Its three marks.** ✅ **Idle talk** (*Gerede*) — passing along received opinions without genuine understanding; **curiosity** (*Neugier*) — flitting restlessly from novelty to novelty; **ambiguity** (*Zweideutigkeit*) — everything seems accessible and nothing is genuinely appropriated. Each is a **deficient mode** of an existentiale rather than a vice.

**Why falling is not a failing.** **Falling** (*Verfallen*) is Dasein's tendency to be absorbed in the world of its concern and in *das Man*. ⚠️ It is expressly **not** a theological "fall from grace" but an **ontological structure**: Dasein is inauthentic **proximally and for the most part** (*zunächst und zumeist*), and this is a structural feature of being-in-the-world, not a deficiency to be repented.

**Consequence.** ✅ Authenticity is therefore a **modification** of this condition, reached through the silent **call of conscience** and **anticipatory resoluteness** — a change in ownership, not in moral quality.

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** the second clause of the question is the marked point; the exposition must be arranged so that the non-moral verdict is earned rather than announced.

- **Structure:** thesis → the "they" → three marks → why not a failing → consequence.
- **Doctrine:** *Mitsein*; deficient modes; falling as structure.
- **Evidence:** *Being and Time* (1927) §§26, 27, 35–38.
- **Distinction:** ontological structure against moral fault.
- **Criticism:** implicit — the formality that makes falling blameless also makes authenticity contentless.

#### Original 3 · 15 marks

**Question:** Critically examine Sartre's claim that conflict is the original meaning of being-for-others.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** The claim follows validly from Sartre's premises, and the premises — above all the exclusivity of subject and object — are where the argument is vulnerable.

**How the Other is given.** ✅ In the keyhole scene I am absorbed pre-reflectively: there is **no ego** in my consciousness and no self to be ashamed. Footsteps transform this instantly: **I am seen**, and I become an object with a nature, judged from outside. **Shame** is by its structure shame **before someone**, so "shame is by nature **recognition**"; the Other is **lived**, not inferred. Hence "the Other is the **indispensable mediator** between myself and me": I acquire an **outside** I did not choose and cannot know from within.

**Why conflict follows.** ⚠️ Objectified by the Other's freedom, I have two options and both fail. **Assimilating** that freedom — love, language, masochism — fails because to be loved is to be made an object, so the very freedom sought is destroyed in the possessing. **Transcending** it — indifference, desire, sadism, hate — fails because an object cannot confer the recognition wanted, and hate seeks a disappearance that would leave my being-for-others intact. Hence *Huis clos* (**1944**): "**hell is other people**".

**The correction.** ✅ Sartre explained in a **1965** recorded preface that the line does not mean relations with others are always poisonous, but that if one's relations are twisted the Other's judgement becomes the medium of self-torment — and that the three characters are **dead**, and so fixed.

**Criticism.** ❓ Three objections. The examples are drawn from a narrow, largely pathological range. Love, trust and being-cared-for seem equally basic and are not objectifications. And **Levinas** argues that to meet the Other only as a rival freedom is still to think him from the self — an objection **unanswered** within *Being and Nothingness*. Sartre's later **group-in-fusion** (1960) suggests the first objection landed.

**Verdict.** The Look gives a compelling non-inferential account of exposure to another perspective; the leap from that to universal conflict depends on the presupposition that one cannot be looking and looked-at at once.

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** "critically examine" requires that the objection attack a **premise**, not merely register discomfort with the conclusion.

- **Structure:** thesis → the Look → why conflict → the correction → three objections → verdict.
- **Doctrine:** shame as recognition; the two failing strategies.
- **Evidence:** *Being and Nothingness* (1943) Part III; *Huis clos* (1944); the 1965 preface; the 1960 *Critique*.
- **Distinction:** ontological claim against statistical generalisation.
- **Criticism:** subject/object exclusivity; Levinas unanswered.

#### Original 4 · 15 marks

**Question:** "Pseudonymity is the formal counterpart of the doctrine that truth is subjectivity." Discuss.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** The statement is correct: the form is **deduced** from the doctrine, and reading pseudonymity as a literary habit removes an argument.

**The argument, in steps.** ✅ (i) Objective truth — historical, scientific, metaphysical — can be transmitted **directly**: I state a result, you receive it, and nothing about your existence need change. (ii) Ethical and religious truth is not a result but a **mode of existing**, an appropriation in inwardness. (iii) Communicated directly, it would be taken over **as a result** — as information one can hold without becoming — and the communication would therefore **falsify** what it communicates. (iv) Hence **indirect communication** (*indirekte Meddelelse*): a form that withholds the result and refuses authority. (v) The model is **Socratic maieutics**, the midwife who "**deceives into the truth**", and the author becomes a **vanishing occasion**. (vi) Hence **pseudonymity**: each pseudonym is a **personified existence-possibility** writing from within a sphere it cannot see beyond.

**The evidence.** ✅ **Johannes de Silentio** admires faith without possessing it; **Johannes Climacus** is explicitly not a Christian, which is why he can pose the problem of becoming one; **Anti-Climacus** stands above Kierkegaard himself. Simultaneously, under his own name, Kierkegaard published the **Upbuilding Discourses** — the **direct pole** of a single strategy. In the "**First and Last Declaration**" (*Postscript*, **1846**) he calls himself the "author's author", a **prompter**, and asks that the pseudonym be quoted.

**Objections.** ❓ The method makes his own position **unattributable**; the reply is the signed pole and *The Point of View*, **written 1848, published posthumously 1859** — itself a disputed retrospective. More sharply, if subjective truth cannot be said directly, the thesis cannot be said directly either; **Climacus concedes this** by **revoking** the *Postscript* at its close.

**Verdict.** The method's **cost** is that it is self-consuming; its **power** is that the cost is intended. Kierkegaard is not evading a position but denying that a position, in Hegel's systematic sense, is the kind of thing an existing individual can hand over.

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** the six numbered steps are the answer's spine; the pseudonym evidence proves the steps rather than replacing them.

- **Structure:** thesis → six-step argument → evidence → objections → verdict.
- **Doctrine:** appropriation; maieutics; existence-possibilities.
- **Evidence:** *Postscript* (1846); the "First and Last Declaration"; *The Point of View* (1848/1859).
- **Distinction:** method against biography.
- **Criticism:** unattributability; the revocation.

#### Original 5 · 20 marks

**Question:** "Anxiety is not a stronger kind of fear." Examine the role of anxiety in Kierkegaard, Heidegger and Sartre, and assess whether a mood can disclose a philosophical truth.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** Fear and anxiety differ **in kind**, not degree, and the shared argument in all three thinkers turns on that difference — though the conclusion each draws is different.

**The general argument.** ⚠️ (i) Moods are not private colourings added to a neutral world; they are ways in which one's situation is **disclosed**. (ii) **Fear** discloses an entity as threatening and keeps us within the world of concern. (iii) **Anxiety** has no entity as its object and therefore cannot disclose an entity. (iv) Yet it plainly discloses something, since it has definite content and is not mere confusion. (v) Therefore what it discloses is a **structure**. (vi) Therefore anxiety is philosophically **privileged**: the one mood in which existence as a whole becomes an issue.

**Kierkegaard.** ✅ *The Concept of Anxiety* (**1844**), by **Vigilius Haufniensis**: "the object of anxiety is a **nothing**"; what appears is **possibility**, "the possibility of being able". Anxiety is "the **dizziness of freedom**", present already **in innocence**, and the **presupposition** of the leap.

**Heidegger.** ✅ *Being and Time* **§40**: in anxiety "the world has the character of **complete insignificance**" — the totality of involvements collapses and Dasein is thrown back on its bare *that-it-is*. That in the face of which and that for which one is anxious **are the same**: **being-in-the-world**. Anxiety discloses Dasein as *solus ipse* and **frees it for authentic choosing**. In "What is Metaphysics?" (**1929**) he adds that it reveals the **Nothing** (*das Nichts*) — "nothing noths", never "noughts".

**Sartre.** ✅ *Being and Nothingness* (1943), Part I. On the **cliff path** I feel fear of the drop, then realise that **nothing prevents me** from throwing myself over — vertigo before my own freedom. The **gambler** finds yesterday's firm resolution **present but powerless**: it must be remade now, and nothing guarantees he will remake it. ⚠️ Anguish is distinct from **nausea**, which discloses the contingency of the in-itself.

**Assessment.** ❓ The naturalist replies that anxiety is a clinical or neurochemical state with no cognitive credentials. The counter is that the existentialist claim is not that anxious people know more, but that the **structure** of the mood — having no object — is itself the datum requiring explanation, and no reduction of it to an unnoticed object has succeeded. **Verdict:** a mood can disclose a structure without becoming infallible cognition; naturalistic and clinical explanations remain compatible competitors rather than refuted rivals, and the three analyses must not be merged into one mood theory.

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** the six-step argument makes the three expositions cumulative rather than parallel; without it the answer becomes three summaries.

- **Structure:** thesis → shared argument → three analyses → naturalist objection → verdict.
- **Doctrine:** objectlessness; possibility, being-in-the-world, freedom.
- **Evidence:** *Concept of Anxiety* (1844); *Being and Time* §40; "What is Metaphysics?" (1929); the cliff path and the gambler.
- **Distinction:** difference in kind; anguish against nausea.
- **Criticism:** the naturalist competitor, met but not defeated.

#### Original 6 · 20 marks

**Question:** Existentialism has been charged with subjectivism, pessimism and social abstraction. Examine these charges with reference to Kierkegaard, Sartre and Heidegger.

##### Independent model answer

**Thesis.** Each charge has a different answer for each thinker, so a single reply on behalf of "existentialism" would answer for none of them; two of the three charges fail as stated, and the third partly succeeds.

**Why the charges must be split.** ⚠️ "Existentialism" is a retrospective family label: Kierkegaard died in **1855** and never met the word, Heidegger denied it in the "Letter on Humanism" (**1946/47**), and only Sartre accepted and defined it (**1945/46**). A movement-level objection is therefore a shape awaiting three different fillings.

**Subjectivism.** ❓ Against **Kierkegaard** the charge misfires: Climacus's thesis concerns the **mode of appropriation**, not the content of belief, and the same author writes "**subjectivity is untruth**", which no relativist could use. Against **Sartre** it misfires differently: choice is bound to **responsibility** and to "in choosing myself, I choose man". Against **Heidegger** it misfires most clearly: he is conducting **fundamental ontology**, and *das Man* is criticised precisely for validating received opinion. ✅ The charge fails as stated.

**Pessimism.** ❓ Anxiety, despair, death and nothingness are **diagnostic and disclosive structures**, not the claim that every life is emotionally miserable. Kierkegaard's despair is a **misrelation** that may be entirely unfelt; Heidegger's being-toward-death is a **possibility** that individualises rather than a morbid preoccupation; Sartre's anguish is the recognition of freedom. ✅ The charge rests on reading technical terms as mood-reports.

**Social abstraction.** ⚠️ Here the critics land a blow. Sartre's early vocabulary under-describes gendered, racialised and material constraint — pressed by **de Beauvoir**, **Fanon** and Marxist critics — and his own *Critique of Dialectical Reason* (**1960**), with **scarcity**, **seriality** and the **practico-inert**, concedes it. Heidegger's **being-with** (§26) is constitutive, but authenticity remains **formally empty**, a charge sharpened by his 1933 complicity and by Adorno's "jargon of authenticity". Kierkegaard's treatment of institutions and community is genuinely thin, and "the crowd is untruth" answers only part of the objection.

**Verdict.** ❓ Subjectivism and pessimism are misdescriptions that dissolve once the technical vocabulary is respected. Social abstraction is a **real residue**: the replies available — situated freedom, being-with, and the individual's irreducible responsibility — are **partial rather than complete**, and the honest closing move is to concede that rather than to argue it away.

##### Why this earns marks

**Answer-writing focus:** the structure — one charge, three replies, then a ruling — is what converts a survey into an examination, and the concession at the end is what makes the earlier defences credible.

- **Structure:** thesis → why split the charges → three charges, three replies each → verdict.
- **Doctrine:** appropriation; responsibility; fundamental ontology; disclosive moods.
- **Evidence:** *Postscript* (1846); "Letter on Humanism" (1946/47); *Critique of Dialectical Reason* (1960); §26.
- **Distinction:** misdescription against genuine residue.
- **Criticism:** formal emptiness; thin community; partial replies conceded.

## OPTIONAL ADVANCED DEPTH — NOT REQUIRED FOR A CORE ANSWER

### Prerequisite check before using this block

⚠️ Nothing below is needed to write a complete answer to any of the fourteen routed 2018–2025 question-parts. Complete Sessions 1–18, the thirty-two diagnostics and the fourteen solved parts first. In a fifteen-mark answer use **at most one** module from this block, and in a twenty-marker **at most two**; each is a second-order interpretive dispute, not a doctrine the core answer rests on.

### A1. How far does a pseudonym speak for Kierkegaard? ❓

- **Core status, stated first.** ✅ Indirect communication and the pseudonymous authorship were **promoted out of optional status on 15 August 2026** and now live in the Core owner. Session 5 is therefore **not** optional background, and this module addresses only the residue.
- **The second-order issue.** Granting Session 5's account, *how far* may a reader treat a pseudonym's thesis as Kierkegaard's own — and does the method leave him with any assertible doctrine at all?
- **Competing interpretations.** ❓ (i) The **authorial-doctrine** reading: the pseudonyms are transparent, and the theses are Kierkegaard's. (ii) The **strict indirect-communication** reading: nothing pseudonymous may be attributed to him at all. (iii) The **mixed** reading: the pseudonyms are dramatised standpoints through which the reader must work, while the signed writings carry first-person religious assertion.
- **Extra objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** if the author speaks through masks, doctrine becomes unstable and unfalsifiable. **Counter-reply:** the instability is deliberate and self-limiting — the pseudonyms stage standpoints the reader must *occupy and exhaust*, while the signed *Upbuilding Discourses* retain direct religious assertion.
- **Safe 15/20 use.** ⚠️ One attribution-caution clause when quoting *Fear and Trembling* or the *Postscript*. Never a replacement for the Core exposition.
- **What not to over-study.** ⚠️ Do not turn the answer into literary biography or editorial history.
- **Promotion status.** ✅ Fired and executed. Re-promote only if a future part asks for the *attribution problem itself*.

### A2. Is Sartrean freedom solitary, or constituted through the Other? ⚠️

- **The issue.** Is freedom in *Being and Nothingness* fundamentally an interior structure, or is it always already exposed to conflict, shame and objectification through others?
- **Competing interpretations.** ❓ (i) The **conflictual social ontology** reading, which takes Part III at its word. (ii) A **softer** reading that finds room for reciprocity in Sartre's own analysis of love and language. (iii) The **later-revision** reading, which treats history and social mediation in the *Critique of Dialectical Reason* (1960) as the position Sartre was moving toward.
- **Extra objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** if "hell is other people", Sartrean freedom becomes theatrically hostile and politically thin. **Counter-reply:** the doctrine is best used to show that freedom is never purely interior — it is lived under the gaze of others and later requires social mediation.
- **Safe 15/20 use.** ⚠️ One enriching sentence in a freedom or bad-faith answer. The core answer must still rest on the for-itself, facticity and choice.
- **What not to over-study.** ⚠️ Do not drag *No Exit* or the later Marxist Sartre into every Paper I answer.
- **Promote to Core if:** a future part directly asks about being-for-others, the Look, or intersubjective conflict.

### A3. Is Heideggerian authenticity decisionism or a formal existential structure? ❓

- **The issue.** Is authenticity a heroic decisionist ideal, or a formally thin owning of one's finite possibilities?
- **Competing interpretations.** ❓ (i) The **heroic/decisionist** reading, in which resoluteness supplies its own justification. (ii) The **formal-existential** reading, in which authenticity modifies ownership and prescribes nothing. (iii) The **political-shadow** reading, influenced by Heidegger's 1933 complicity, which treats the formality itself as the doctrine's political liability.
- **Extra objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** authenticity can look morally empty or politically dangerous once detached from norms. **Counter-reply:** in *Being and Time* authenticity is not a programme or a virtue-code; it is a mode of owning finitude, guilt and death instead of dissolving into *das Man*.
- **Safe 15/20 use.** ⚠️ A closing nuance in a Heidegger question on authenticity or temporality — one sentence, after the Core verdict.
- **What not to over-study.** ⚠️ Do not let biography displace the ontological text unless the question invites the move.
- **Promote to Core if:** a future part directly asks whether authenticity is ethical, political or merely ontological.

### A4. What the promotion record shows ⚠️

One module in this clause has already **fired**: pseudonymity and indirect communication migrated into the Core owner on 15 August 2026 and are now marks-essential. The two remaining modules are **bounded enrichment** and should be promoted only if a future paper asks for them directly. The practical lesson is that the boundary between "core" and "advanced" in this clause is set by the **PYQ corpus**, not by intrinsic interest: a doctrine becomes compulsory the moment a question makes it answer-necessary.

### Conceptual links — not current-affairs items

⚠️ No dated official or academic development is claimed for this clause. Its live relevance is the standing philosophical agenda: the analysis of autonomy and manipulation, the ethics of authenticity in professional and public life, the philosophy of moods and their cognitive standing, phenomenological approaches to embodiment and finitude in medicine and psychiatry, and the continuing use of facticity and situated freedom in feminist and postcolonial theory. Each of these is **analytical**, carries no sourced factual claim, and should never be presented as a current-affairs anchor.

### Source trail for this block

Kierkegaard, *Either/Or*, *Fear and Trembling*, *Concluding Unscientific Postscript*, *The Point of View for My Work as an Author*; Sartre, *Being and Nothingness*, *Existentialism Is a Humanism*, *Critique of Dialectical Reason*; Heidegger, *Being and Time*, "Letter on Humanism"; and the secondary trail of Hubert Dreyfus, William Blattner, Steven Crowell, Joseph Catalano and Thomas Flynn.

## CONSOLIDATED REGISTER NOTES

> The compressed revision layer. Eighteen parts, A to R, running against the eighteen teaching sessions, followed by the complete ASCII master flow diagram. Read the parts for recall and redraw the diagram from memory as the final check.

### A. The clause, its thinkers and what is not owned

- **Syllabus, verbatim:** *Existentialism (Kierkegaard, Sarte, Heidegger): Existence and Essence; Choice, Responsibility and Authentic Existence; Being-in-the-world and Temporality.* 📝 "Sarte" is a printed misspelling of **Sartre**.
- **Family label, not a school.** Kierkegaard d. **1855**, never met the word; Heidegger **denied** it ("Letter on Humanism", written 1946, published **1947**); Sartre **accepted and defined** it (lecture Oct **1945**, published **1946**).
- **Three names, three concepts:** the **single individual** (*den Enkelte*) · the **for-itself** (*pour-soi*) · **Dasein**.
- **God:** central (K) · denied **as a premise** (S) · not settled by the ontology (H).
- **Limb 3** — being-in-the-world and temporality — is almost entirely **Heidegger's**.
- **Corpus:** **14 of 112** owned parts, **joint-highest**, at least one in **every year 2018–2025**. Sartre 8, Kierkegaard 4, Heidegger 3.
- **Not owned:** Husserl's method, essences and anti-psychologism as doctrine (**Topic 09**) · Hegel's dialectic and Absolute Spirit (**Topic 05**) · Descartes' cogito and dualism (**Topic 02**) · Kant on freedom and the antinomies (**Topic 04**) · **Nietzsche entirely** · Camus, Jaspers, Marcel and de Beauvoir as systems · existentialist theology and immortality (**Paper II**).

### B. Provenance and chronology — examiner-checked

- **1843** *Either/Or* (Victor Eremita) · *Fear and Trembling* (Johannes de Silentio) · *Repetition* (Constantin Constantius).
- **1844** *The Concept of Anxiety* (Vigilius Haufniensis) — Lowrie: *The Concept of Dread* · *Philosophical Fragments* (Climacus).
- **1846** *Concluding Unscientific Postscript* (Climacus) + the **"First and Last Declaration"**.
- **1848/1859** *The Point of View* — **written 1848, published posthumously 1859**; retrospective and disputed.
- **1849** *The Sickness unto Death* (Anti-Climacus) · **1850** *Practice in Christianity*.
- **1927** *Being and Time* — **unfinished**; Division III and Part Two never published. Cite by **§**.
- **1929** "What is Metaphysics?" — the **Nothing**; "**Nothing noths**", ❌ never "noughts".
- **1943** *Being and Nothingness* — tr. Barnes 1956, Richmond 2018.
- **1944** *Huis clos* — "l'enfer, c'est les autres"; corrected by Sartre in a **1965** recorded preface.
- **1945/1946** *Existentialism Is a Humanism* — **all the slogans**; Sartre regretted publishing it.
- **1946/1947** "Letter on Humanism" — the repudiation. **1960** *Critique of Dialectical Reason*.
- **Translations:** Macquarrie & Robinson 1962 / Stambaugh 1996 (rev. Schmidt 2010); Hong & Hong 1978–2000 / Lowrie / Hannay.
- **Attribution rule:** cite the **pseudonym**, per the printed request; Kierkegaard is only the "author's author", a **prompter**.

### C. Kierkegaard: the existing individual and truth as subjectivity

- Target: **Hegel's System** — Absolute Spirit, **mediation**, the individual as a moment.
- Counter: **"A logical system is possible; an existential system is impossible."** No system contains the one who builds it.
- **"Truth is subjectivity"** — **Johannes Climacus**, *Postscript* **1846**: the **how** of appropriation (infinite passionate inwardness, commitment, risk) for **ethical/religious** truth.
- **Three denials:** not relativism · not a denial of objective truth · not "facts vary with passion" — only **insufficiency**.
- **Complement:** **"subjectivity is untruth"** — sin, finitude, the need of the leap. A relativist could not use it.
- **"The crowd is untruth"** — distributed responsibility is borne by no one.
- **Contrasts:** either/or vs both/and · the leap vs mediation · indirect communication vs the lecture.
- **Verdict:** exposes what a system-view loses; does not refute Hegel, offers no rival system.

### D. Kierkegaard: the three spheres and the leap

- **Aesthetic** — the **interesting**, immediacy, irony, no binding commitment → **boredom**, fragmentation. *Either/Or* (1843), "A".
- **Ethical** — **universal duty**, marriage, vocation, transparency → **guilt**. Judge William.
- **Religious** — before God as a singular individual, faith as "the absurd" → **paradox**. *Fear and Trembling* (1843), **de Silentio**.
- **Teleological suspension of the ethical** — Abraham; the text **stages the paradox**, it does not license private revelation.
- **Two knights:** **infinite resignation** (visible renunciation) vs **faith** (invisible; double movement; lives fully in the finite).
- **Transition = qualitative leap (*Springet*)** — not mediation, not maturation; **relapse always possible**.
- ⚠️ "**Leap of faith**" is the **English tradition's** fused phrase.
- The spheres are the **content** of which "truth is subjectivity" is the **form**.

### E. Kierkegaard: anxiety and despair

- **Anxiety** (*Angest*), **Vigilius Haufniensis**, **1844**: object is "**a nothing**"; discloses **possibility**, "the possibility of being able".
- **"The dizziness of freedom"**; "a **sympathetic antipathy and an antipathetic sympathy**"; the abyss — dizziness **in the eye**.
- Present already **in innocence**; the **presupposition** of the leap, not its cause.
- **Despair**, **Anti-Climacus**, **1849**: the self is **a relation relating itself to itself** and thereby to **the power that established it**; despair is a **misrelation** — and may be **unfelt**.
- Two forms: **not willing to be oneself** · **defiantly willing to be oneself** without the God-relation.
- ⚠️ Kierkegaardian despair ≠ **Sartrean *désespoir*** (not relying on outcomes beyond one's action).

### F. Kierkegaard: indirect communication and pseudonymity

- **Promoted to Core, 15 Aug 2026** — marks-essential for subjectivity and anti-Hegel questions.
- Six steps: objective truth transmissible → existential truth is a **mode of existing** → direct transmission **falsifies** → **indirect communication** → **Socratic maieutics**, "**deceives into the truth**", **vanishing occasion** → **pseudonymity**.
- Pseudonyms as **existence-possibilities**: **Eremita** · **de Silentio** (admires faith, lacks it) · **Constantius** · **Haufniensis** · **Climacus** (not a Christian) · **Anti-Climacus** (above the author).
- **Double authorship**: the signed *Upbuilding Discourses* are the **direct pole**.
- **"First and Last Declaration"** (1846): "author's author", **prompter** (*souffleur*), quote the pseudonym.
- Objections: **unattributability** (reply: signed pole + *Point of View*, itself disputed) · **self-undermining** (reply: Climacus **revokes** the *Postscript*) · Hegelian sublation.

### G. Sartre: existence precedes essence

- **Paper-knife** (*coupe-papier*): artisan → design → object, so **essence precedes existence** for artefacts; theism applied this to man.
- **Atheism is a PREMISE**: no divine artisan → no antecedent blueprint.
- "Man first of all exists, encounters himself, surges up in the world — and defines himself afterwards."
- ⚠️ **Facticity constrains**; "man is nothing else but what he makes of himself" is the **lecture's** formula and must be balanced by **situation**.
- Provenance: lecture **Oct 1945**, published **1946**; *Being and Nothingness* is **1943**; Sartre **regretted** the lecture.
- **Heidegger repudiates** it (1946/47): **§9** is **ontological**; Sartre merely **reverses** the priority and stays in the **metaphysics of subjectivity**; man is the **shepherd of Being**.
- **Kierkegaard** never wrote it and could not accept the premise.
- Objection: **decisionism** / no value-criteria (Murdoch; Marxists).

### H. Sartre: in-itself and for-itself

- **En-soi**: full, solid, **self-identical**, opaque — "**it is what it is**"; contingent, "**de trop**" (root of **nausea**).
- **Pour-soi**: nihilating, self-surpassing, never self-identical — "**it is what it is not and is not what it is**".
- **"Is what it is not"** = ahead of itself, defined by a **future** that does not exist. **"Is not what it is"** = cannot coincide with **past/facticity** (the waiter).
- **Nihilation** (*néantisation*): consciousness stands back and negates — questions, imagines, projects. The for-itself is "the being by which **nothingness** comes into the world".
- **Impossible synthesis**: *pour-soi-en-soi* = **God** = contradiction → "**man is a useless passion**".
- ⚠️ **Ontological**, not mind–matter. Objection: is non-self-identity coherent? **Contested.**

### I. Sartre: freedom, choice and the three moods

- Freedom is **what the for-itself is**, not what it has. "**Man is condemned to be free**" (1945/46). "Thrown" is **Heidegger's** word.
- **Non-choice is choice**: "I can always choose, but if I do not choose, I am still choosing" — usually **bad faith**.
- **Anguish** (*angoisse*) — vertigo before my own freedom. **Abandonment** (*délaissement*) — no *a priori* values in an "intelligible heaven". **Despair** (*désespoir*) — no reliance on outcomes beyond my action.
- "**In choosing myself, I choose man**" — quasi-Kantian universalisability; source of the **weight** and the anguish.
- Tension: universalisability against the denial of objective values; reply = the **form** is inherent in free choice; *Notebooks for an Ethics* unfinished.
- Two boundaries: freedom is **situated**; "**no excuses**" = responsibility for taking up motives.

### J. Sartre: facticity, transcendence and bad faith

- **Facticity** = body, past, social position, situation. **Transcendence** = freedom to negate and project.
- **Bad faith** (*mauvaise foi*) = the **collapse of the tension** into one dimension — not ordinary lying.
- **The waiter** — denial that a role **exhausts** the for-itself; role-performance alone is **not automatically** bad faith.
- **The woman on the date** — the **double lie**: denies her **transcendence** (a body-object) *and* the **facticity** of the gesture (treated as neutral).
- **Paradox**: must know and not know. **Freudian unconscious rejected** → **pre-reflective, non-thetic** awareness in a non-self-identical consciousness. ❓ Contested (Fingarette).
- 2018 chain: **bad faith (project) → inauthenticity (mode) → alienation (cost)**.
- Everyday form: the **serious spirit**. Residue: **no completed positive doctrine of authenticity**.

### K. Sartre: freedom and determinism

- A **motive** guides action only through the **meaning** conferred on it; conferral is an act of the for-itself → **no motive determines unless constituted as one**.
- Determinism misdescribes the *pour-soi* as an *en-soi*: the problem is **false**.
- "**You can always make something out of what you have been made into**": facticity **conceded** + transcendence **asserted** + "always" is **structural**.
- Objection: oppression limits **action and the cognitive range** (Merleau-Ponty; Marxists; **de Beauvoir**, **Fanon**).
- Reply: freedom of **consciousness** vs freedom of **action** — real, but **weakening**.
- **Critique of Dialectical Reason 1960**: **scarcity**, **seriality**, **practico-inert**, **group-in-fusion** — a **concession**, never the doctrine of 1943.

### L. Sartre: the Look and being-for-others

- **Keyhole**: absorbed (**no ego**, pre-reflective, nothing to be ashamed of) → **caught** → an **outside** is conferred → **shame**.
- "**Shame is by nature recognition**"; "**the Other is the indispensable mediator between myself and me**".
- Printed subterms: *le regard* · *l'être-pour-autrui* · *la honte* · *le corps-pour-autrui* · *conflit*.
- Two failing strategies: **assimilate** (love, language, masochism) — being loved is being made an object; **transcend** (indifference, desire, sadism, hate) — an object cannot confer recognition; hate seeks a disappearance that leaves being-for-others intact.
- "**Conflict is the original meaning of being-for-others**"; *Huis clos* **1944**; Sartre's **1965** correction — the characters are **dead**, i.e. fixed.
- **P1–P4**, esp. **P3 subject/object exclusivity**; the "we-subject" is discussed and rejected.
- Four accounts: **Husserl** (pairing, appresentation) · **Heidegger** (*Mitsein*) · **Sartre** (the Look) · **Levinas** (the **face**, ethical asymmetry) — **unanswered** in 1943.
- ⚠️ **Bounded content**: one development, never the spine of a freedom answer.

### M. Heidegger: the Being-question and the break with the transcendental ego

- Project = **fundamental ontology**: the **meaning of Being**, not an existentialist psychology.
- **Ontological difference**: **Being** (intelligibility) ≠ **beings**; Being is **not a supreme entity**.
- Dasein chosen because it has a **pre-ontological understanding of Being** and **its own Being is at issue**.
- **§9**: "the essence of Dasein lies in its existence" → **existentialia**, not categories.
- ***Epoché*** = **Husserl's** suspension of the existence-posit, residue = transcendental subjectivity — **one accurate sentence**, Topic 09.
- Objection: the reduction **begins from reflection** and presupposes a spectator; the **primordial** condition is **already-involved being-in-the-world**; **subject and object are derivative**.
- ⚠️ He **redirects** description, does not abandon it. ❌ Not spatial containment; ❌ not Sartrean consciousness.
- *Being and Time* **1927** is **unfinished**; the **turn** (*Kehre*) followed.

### N. Heidegger: being-in-the-world and worldhood

- **Unitary** structure; the hyphens block decomposition; the Cartesian bridge problem is **dissolved**, not solved.
- **Worldhood** = **referential context of meaning**, not the totality of objects.
- **Ready-to-hand** (*Zuhandenheit*) is **primary**; **present-at-hand** (*Vorhandenheit*) is **derivative** — a **modification** of practical engagement.
- **In-order-to** (*Um-zu*) chain: hammer → nail → board → house → **dwelling**, a possibility of Dasein's own being. There is no such thing as one piece of equipment.
- **Breakdown**: **unusability · absence · obstruction** → the network becomes **conspicuous**; ⚠️ significance is **not** erased.

### O. Heidegger: the existentialia and care

- **Attunement** (*Befindlichkeit*) — always already in a **mood** (*Stimmung*); mood **discloses**, never colours; even indifference is a mood.
- **Understanding** (*Verstehen*) — **projection upon possibilities**; practical know-how, not cognition.
- **Discourse** (*Rede*) — the **articulation** of understanding; condition of speech and listening.
- Together = **disclosedness** (*Erschlossenheit*) = the **"there"** (*Da*).
- **Care** (*Sorge*, **§41**): "**ahead-of-itself — already-in — alongside**" = projection · thrownness/attunement · concern/falling.
- ⚠️ **Ontological**, not psychological: it makes worry **and** indifference possible.
- **Concern** (*Besorgen*, equipment) vs **solicitude** (*Fürsorge*, other Dasein); **being-with** (*Mitsein*, **§26**) is **constitutive**.

### P. Heidegger: *das Man*, death and authenticity

- Authenticity/inauthenticity = **existential modifications**, not moral categories; inauthenticity is the **default** — *zunächst und zumeist* — and is **structural**.
- ***Das Man*** is the impersonal **anyone**, not "other people": **idle talk** (*Gerede*) · **curiosity** (*Neugier*) · **ambiguity** (*Zweideutigkeit*), each a **deficient mode**.
- **Falling** (*Verfallen*) — ontological structure, **not** a fall from grace.
- **Call of conscience** (**§§54–60**) — **silent**, **no rule**, returns Dasein to its **null** responsibility.
- **Death** (**§§46–53**) — **ownmost · non-relational · certain · indefinite as to when**; a **possibility**, not an event. Inauthentic: "**one dies**" (*man stirbt*).
- **Anticipatory resoluteness** (*vorlaufende Entschlossenheit*) → existence **as a whole**; individualisation **modifies**, does not abolish, **being-with**.
- Four things it is not: moral goodness · "true to yourself" · individualism/withdrawal · fear of dying.
- Objections: **formal emptiness** (1933; **Adorno's** "jargon of authenticity") · **Levinas** (the Other) · **Arendt** (**natality**).

### Q. Heidegger: temporality and historicality

- **Temporality is the meaning of care** — Division Two's central thesis.
- **Ahead-of-itself → future** (*Zukunft*) · **already-in → having-been** (*Gewesenheit*) · **alongside → present** (*Gegenwart*).
- ⚠️ *Gewesenheit* = the past Dasein **still is**, not elapsed time.
- **Ecstases**, not segments: Dasein stands out into all three **simultaneously**; temporality is a **finite unity**.
- **Priority of the future**; authenticity is **futural**; anticipatory resoluteness gathers existence **as a whole**.
- **Ordinary time** (*innerzeitige Zeit*) = infinite **now-series**, clock-measured, **derived** via **world-time and public dating**.
- ⚠️ "**Vulgar**" = a **levelled interpretation**, **not** a moral defect; clock-time is indispensable.
- **Historicality** (*Geschichtlichkeit*): possibilities are **inherited** and **projected**.
- **Division III never published**.

### R. Fear and anxiety, the criticism ledger and exam craft

- **Fear**: determinate object; flees **toward** the world. **Anxiety**: **no object**; **individualises**; discloses a **structure**.
- **K**: possibility, "dizziness of freedom". **H (§40)**: "**complete insignificance**"; in-the-face-of-which = for-which = **being-in-the-world**; *solus ipse*; **1929** the **Nothing**. **S**: **cliff path** and **gambler**; anguish ≠ **nausea**.
- Six-step argument: moods disclose → fear discloses an entity → anxiety has none → yet has content → so a **structure** → hence **privileged**. ❓ Naturalist objection met, not defeated.
- **Sartre ledger**: implausibility (conceded 1960) · bad-faith coherence · **decisionism** · universalisability tension · constraint.
- **Heidegger ledger**: **formal emptiness** · unfinished Division III · Levinas/Arendt · obscurity (Carnap 1932) · political ambiguity.
- **Kierkegaard ledger**: **fideism** · thin institutions/community (**genuine weakness**) · elitism · relativism (a **misreading**).
- **Movement-level**: subjectivism (three replies) · pessimism (**disclosive structures**) · social abstraction (**partial** replies — concede the residue).
- **Debate grid**: God · core term · path · freedom · temporality · enemy · Hegel · mood.
- **Directive fidelity**: statement questions → **locate in the thinker's system first**; "critically" → assessment **dominates**; verdicts are **graded 10/15/20**.
- **Four cheap marks**: cite by **§**, name the **translation**, date the **slogans** to 1945/46, quote the **pseudonym**.
- ❌ Never: "the existentialists held…" · "authenticity = being true to yourself" · "*Being and Time* is complete" · "leap of faith" as Kierkegaard's · "nothing noughts" · the slogan as Heidegger's.

            'Condemned to be free', 'existence precedes essence' and 'man is
            nothing else but what he makes of himself' ALL come from the
            LECTURE of October 1945, PUBLISHED 1946 - NOT from Being and
            Nothingness (1943).
  FORBIDDEN: 'leap of faith' as Kierkegaard's own phrase; 'nothing noughts';
  'Being and Time completes the analysis of Being'.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 6/53: KIERKEGAARD - THE TARGET AND THE STRUCTURAL COUNTER
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  THE TARGET: HEGEL'S SYSTEM, AS KIERKEGAARD RECEIVES IT
    Reality is the self-development of ABSOLUTE SPIRIT (Geist).
    Every opposition is MEDIATED (aufgehoben) into a higher unity.
    The individual is a MOMENT in a total rational process.
    'The truth is the whole.'
    FENCE: the dialectic and Absolute Spirit belong to TOPIC 05.  Two clauses
    of target-setting here, then the counter.
                                   |
  THE COUNTER - STRUCTURAL, NOT A COMPLAINT ABOUT DIFFICULTY
    The system explains everything EXCEPT THE EXISTING INDIVIDUAL WHO IS
    SUPPOSED TO UNDERSTAND IT.
    To exist is to be PARTICULAR, TEMPORAL, PASSIONATE and DECIDING - none of
    which is a category the system can accommodate without CANCELLING it.
    THE SENTENCE TO DEPLOY:
      'A LOGICAL SYSTEM IS POSSIBLE; AN EXISTENTIAL SYSTEM IS IMPOSSIBLE.'
    NO SYSTEM CAN CONTAIN THE ONE WHO BUILDS IT: the systematiser must still
    EXIST, CHOOSE and DIE.
                                   |
  THE THREE CONTRAST PAIRS THAT CARRY IT
    EITHER/OR         vs  BOTH/AND     to exist IS to choose one thing
                                       rather than another
    THE LEAP          vs  MEDIATION    no argument delivers me from one
    (Springet)                         sphere to the next
    INDIRECT          vs  THE LECTURE  the form must not hand over a result
    COMMUNICATION
                                   |
  BOUNDED VERDICT
    Kierkegaard EXPOSES a first-person appropriation that a completed system-
    view risks LOSING.  He does NOT refute Hegel's ontology and offers NO
    rival comprehensive system.  A Hegelian may reply that the existing
    individual is an unreflective stage philosophy SUBLATES.
  PYQ ANCHORS: 2023 Q1(d) 10m; 2020 Q3(a) 20m; 2022 Q4(a) 20m.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 7/53: KIERKEGAARD - 'TRUTH IS SUBJECTIVITY' AND ITS THREE DENIALS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ATTRIBUTION
    Argued by the pseudonym JOHANNES CLIMACUS in the CONCLUDING UNSCIENTIFIC
    POSTSCRIPT, 1846.  Climacus is a HUMORIST who says explicitly that he is
    NOT A CHRISTIAN - which is exactly why he can pose the problem of
    BECOMING one.
                                   |
  WHAT IT ASSERTS
    For ETHICAL and RELIGIOUS truth the MODE OF APPROPRIATION is decisive:
    HOW one holds a belief - with INFINITE PASSIONATE INWARDNESS, personal
    COMMITMENT, RISK - is the locus of truth FOR THE EXISTING SUBJECT.
    WORKED CONTRAST: to 'know' that God exists as an item in a philosophical
    system is NOT the same as EXISTING BEFORE GOD in faith and trembling.
                                   |
  THREE DENIALS - WRITE ALL THREE
    (i)   NOT relativism.  It is not the claim that anything I believe is
          true.
    (ii)  NOT a denial of objective truth in logic, mathematics or history -
          only of its SUFFICIENCY for existential and religious truth.
    (iii) NOT a general theory that FACTUAL truth varies with the passion of
          the believer.
                                   |
  WHY THE FORM FOLLOWS
    If existential truth is a MODE OF EXISTING rather than a RESULT, it
    cannot be handed over directly without being FALSIFIED - which is why
    Kierkegaard writes PSEUDONYMOUSLY and INDIRECTLY (Panels 13-14).
  PYQ ANCHORS: 2020 Q3(a) 20m; 2022 Q4(a) 20m; 2024 Q1(e) 10m.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 8/53: KIERKEGAARD - THE ANTI-RELATIVIST COMPLEMENT
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  'SUBJECTIVITY IS UNTRUTH'
    The dialectical complement, also Climacus.  The existing subject is ALSO
    IN UNTRUTH - in SIN, in FINITUDE, in NON-IDENTITY WITH THE ETERNAL - and
    therefore NEEDS THE LEAP.
    A RELATIVIST HAS NO USE FOR THIS SENTENCE.  CLIMACUS CANNOT DO WITHOUT
    IT.  That is the INTERNAL EVIDENCE against the relativist reading, and it
    should be quoted in every subjectivity answer.
                                   |
  'THE CROWD IS UNTRUTH'
    The SOCIAL form of the same point.  The anonymous public permits ETHICAL
    EVASION, because a responsibility DISTRIBUTED among everyone is BORNE BY
    NO ONE.  Authentic existence requires the individual to stand ALONE -
    before God, or before the ethical demand.
                                   |
  DO NOT MERGE WITH HEIDEGGER'S DAS MAN
    STRUCTURALLY PARALLEL, DOCTRINALLY DIFFERENT.
      KIERKEGAARD  target = evasion of RESPONSIBILITY BEFORE GOD
      HEIDEGGER    target = a LEVELLING OF POSSIBILITIES, with NO
                   theological content and NO blame attached
  TRAP: writing that Kierkegaard's crowd and Heidegger's das Man are 'the
  same idea in different words' collapses a theological category into an
  ontological one.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 9/53: KIERKEGAARD - THE THREE SPHERES AND THEIR COLLAPSES
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SPHERE      CORE / MODE                    LIMIT - HOW IT COLLAPSES
  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  AESTHETIC   living for the moment: the     BOREDOM, FRAGMENTATION,
  Either/Or   INTERESTING, enjoyment, the    DESPAIR.  Nothing binds, so
  1843, ed.   avoidance of binding           nothing holds the self
  Victor      commitment; immediacy,         together
  Eremita     detachment, IRONY
  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ETHICAL     living by UNIVERSAL DUTY:      GUILT.  The ethical demand
  Judge       commitment, marriage,          cannot be perfectly fulfilled,
  William     vocation, transparency; the    and the UNIVERSAL cannot
              self as responsible agent      accommodate the individual's
              under universal categories     anguished singularity
  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RELIGIOUS   standing BEFORE GOD as a       PARADOX.  Faith is not
  Johannes    singular individual; faith     rational, not universal, not
  de          as 'THE ABSURD'; infinite      mediated by the crowd or the
  Silentio,   passion                        church
  Fear and
  Trembling
  1843
  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  THEY ARE QUALITATIVE SPHERES, NOT CHRONOLOGICAL STAGES.
    One does NOT 'grow out of' the aesthetic by maturation.
    RELAPSE IS ALWAYS POSSIBLE.
    The English word 'stages' is standard in translation, which is exactly
    why the qualification must be written explicitly.
  THE SPHERES ARE THE CONTENT OF WHICH 'TRUTH IS SUBJECTIVITY' IS THE FORM:
  appropriation INTENSIFIES from dispersion through duty to the absolute
  relation.  2022 Q4(a) asks for both, in that order.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 10/53: KIERKEGAARD - THE LEAP, THE SUSPENSION, AND THE TWO KNIGHTS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  THE LEAP (Springet)
    Transition between spheres is a QUALITATIVE CHOICE - not a Hegelian
    deduction, not a psychological development.
    PROVENANCE CAUTION: Kierkegaard writes of THE LEAP and of FAITH.  The
    fused English phrase 'LEAP OF FAITH' belongs to the ENGLISH TRADITION.
    Write: 'the leap - what the English tradition calls the leap of faith.'
                                   |
  THE TELEOLOGICAL SUSPENSION OF THE ETHICAL
    Analysed by JOHANNES DE SILENTIO in FEAR AND TREMBLING (1843) through
    ABRAHAM, commanded to sacrifice Isaac.  The ETHICAL (the universal) is
    SUSPENDED in relation to a higher TELOS - the absolute relation to the
    Absolute.
    THE TEXT STAGES THE PARADOX OF FAITH.  It must NOT be cited as
    Kierkegaard's straightforward LICENCE for private revelation to override
    morality.
    'DE SILENTIO' = the SILENCE of one who ADMIRES faith but DOES NOT HAVE
    it - he can DESCRIBE Abraham but not UNDERSTAND him.
                                   |
  THE TWO KNIGHTS - THE DIFFERENCE IS THE DOCTRINE
    KNIGHT OF INFINITE RESIGNATION   gives the finite UP, and the
                                     renunciation SHOWS.  Visibly heroic,
                                     visibly apart from ordinary life.
    KNIGHT OF FAITH                  gives it up AND RECEIVES IT BACK.
                                     OUTWARDLY INDISTINGUISHABLE from a
                                     bourgeois citizen: lives FULLY in the
                                     finite world WHILE relating absolutely
                                     to the Absolute.
    THE DOUBLE MOVEMENT: infinite resignation FIRST, THEN faith - which is
    why faith is HIGHER than resignation, not easier.
    USE: the fastest available refutation of 'existentialism is world-
    denying'.
  OBJECTION: FIDEISM / IRRATIONALISM.  REPLY: reason is not WRONG but
  LIMITED - it cannot deliver what the religious individual needs.  Whether
  that is a reductio or a valid critique of rationalism DEPENDS ON ONE'S
  EPISTEMOLOGY.  Leave the verdict open.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 11/53: KIERKEGAARD - ANXIETY AS THE DIZZINESS OF FREEDOM
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SOURCE  THE CONCEPT OF ANXIETY, 1844, by VIGILIUS HAUFNIENSIS ('watchman of
          Copenhagen'), writing as a PSYCHOLOGICAL OBSERVER and deliberately
          NOT as a dogmatician.  Lowrie's title: THE CONCEPT OF DREAD.
                                   |
  OBJECT ............ NONE.  'THE OBJECT OF ANXIETY IS A NOTHING.'
  WHAT APPEARS ...... POSSIBILITY - 'the possibility of being able'
  FORMULA ........... 'ANXIETY IS THE DIZZINESS OF FREEDOM'
  AMBIVALENCE ....... 'a SYMPATHETIC ANTIPATHY and an ANTIPATHETIC SYMPATHY'
                      - we are DRAWN to what we SHRINK from
  CANONICAL EXAMPLE . the man at the edge of an abyss grows dizzy - but
                      'THE DIZZINESS IS AS MUCH IN THE EYE AS IN THE ABYSS'.
                      The vertigo is not produced by the drop but by the
                      disclosure that NOTHING PREVENTS HIM
  IN INNOCENCE ...... there is NO knowledge of good and evil, yet anxiety is
                      ALREADY there, because freedom's possibility is already
                      sensed
  ROLE .............. anxiety is the PRESUPPOSITION of the LEAP by which sin
                      - and selfhood - enters.  NOT its CAUSE: a caused leap
                      would not be a leap
                                   |
  TRAP: defining anxiety as INTENSE FEAR.  The difference is IN KIND, not
  degree - fear has a DETERMINATE OBJECT, anxiety has NONE (Panel 33).


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 12/53: KIERKEGAARD - DESPAIR AS A MISRELATION IN THE SELF
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SOURCE  THE SICKNESS UNTO DEATH, 1849, by ANTI-CLIMACUS - a Christian at an
          EXTRAORDINARILY HIGH level, ABOVE Kierkegaard himself, and the
          deliberate MIRROR IMAGE of Climacus, who says he is NOT a Christian
                                   |
  THE DEFINITION
    THE SELF IS NOT A THING.  It is A RELATION THAT RELATES ITSELF TO ITSELF
    - and, in relating to itself, RELATES TO THE POWER THAT ESTABLISHED IT.
    DESPAIR = A MISRELATION IN THAT RELATION.
    NOT an emotion.  NOT sadness.  A STRUCTURAL FAULT THAT MAY BE ENTIRELY
    UNFELT - which is the most examinable feature of the doctrine, because it
    is what allows the claim that despair is universal to be STRUCTURAL
    rather than an empirical exaggeration.
                                   |
  TWO FORMS
    FORM 1  NOT WILLING TO BE ONESELF - fleeing the self one is; wanting to
            be someone else, or no one
    FORM 2  DEFIANTLY WILLING TO BE ONESELF - insisting on being oneself
            WITHOUT the grounding God-relation.  THE DEEPER FORM, precisely
            because it looks like STRENGTH
                                   |
  THE FENCE THAT MUST BE STATED
    KIERKEGAARDIAN DESPAIR (theological: a misrelation before God)
        IS NOT
    SARTREAN DESPAIR (desespoir, 1946 lecture: acting without relying on
        outcomes beyond one's control and reasonable probabilities)
    SAME ENGLISH WORD.  TWO UNRELATED DOCTRINES.


ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 13/53: KIERKEGAARD - INDIRECT COMMUNICATION IN SIX STEPS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  CORE STATUS: promoted OUT of optional-enrichment status on 15 AUGUST 2026.
  Marks-essential for any question on subjectivity or the critique of Hegel.
                                   |
  1  OBJECTIVE TRUTH - historical, scientific, metaphysical - can be
     transmitted DIRECTLY.  I state a result, you receive it, and NOTHING
     ABOUT YOUR EXISTENCE NEED CHANGE.
                                   |
  2  ETHICAL AND RELIGIOUS TRUTH is not a result but A MODE OF EXISTING, an
     APPROPRIATION IN INWARDNESS (Climacus, Postscript 1846).
                                   |
  3  IF such truth were communicated DIRECTLY, the recipient would take it
     over AS A RESULT - as information, as doctrine, as something one can
     KNOW WITHOUT BECOMING.  THE COMMUNICATION WOULD THEREBY FALSIFY EXACTLY
     WHAT IT COMMUNICATES.   <-- THE LOAD-BEARING STEP
                                   |
  4  THEREFORE ethical-religious truth requires INDIRECT COMMUNICATION
     (indirekte Meddelelse): a form that WITHHOLDS THE RESULT, REFUSES TO BE
     AN AUTHORITY, and forces the reader to relate to the possibility FOR
     HIMSELF.
                                   |
  5  THE MODEL IS SOCRATIC MAIEUTICS - the midwife who brings to birth what
     the other must produce, and who, in Kierkegaard's phrase, 'DECEIVES INTO
     THE TRUTH'.  The author must become a VANISHING OCCASION, not a teacher
     with a doctrine.  The 'deception' is FORMAL, not moral: the reader is
     denied a result FOR HIS OWN SAKE.
                                   |
  6  THEREFORE PSEUDONYMITY.  The pseudonyms are NOT disguises for a shy
     author but PERSONIFIED EXISTENCE-POSSIBILITIES, each writing from INSIDE
     a sphere of existence it CANNOT SEE BEYOND.
